On ScanAndAdd, a synthetic task whose joint distribution is available in closed form, we verify that every group of two or more undetermined positions a confidence ranking writes is dependent, and measure the generated distribution to be $29\times$ the sampling-noise floor total variation while per-sample metrics are $1.0$.

</details>


### [257] [PAA: The Probabilistic Allen Algebra: A Generative and Complete Probabilistic Extension of Allen's Interval Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20634)
*Julian Eggert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率Allen代数（PAA），是对Allen区间代数的生成式与完备扩展。它将十三种基础时间关系从精确区间边界的布尔谓词推广为概率关系，其中关系概率由区间边界的分布推导而来，而非直接赋予得分。时间点为高斯分布，区间具有高斯中点和截断高斯时长。所有关系通过线性不等式在共同概率空间中将区间关系归约为多元高斯象限概率，接触关系（meets/starts/finishes/equals）通过容忍带获得正测度；在单一容忍度下，十三种关系构成真划分，且在容忍度趋零时退化为经典Allen代数。该构造演绎而非预设Allen分类体系：粗粒度谓词（先于、重叠、包含）是叶关系的并集，且该层级在区间坍缩为点时保持，十三种关系依次约简为五种、三种。该代数具有尺度不变性，可区分渐变表达（如"shortly before"）与接触关系，并已通过蒙特卡洛验证，开源为测试完备的Python包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Allen区间代数的十三种基础关系是对精确区间边界的清晰谓词，无法刻画来自语言、感知、数据库或不确定历史中的时间信息——这些场景下时间、时长和边界本身不确定，且"just before""roughly during"等表达具有渐变（分级）意义。因此需要一种能表达不确定时间关系的概率化扩展。

Method: 构建概率Allen代数作为Allen代数的生成式和完备扩展。核心思想：关系概率由区间边界分布推导而非直接赋分。时间点为高斯分布；区间由高斯中点与截断高斯时长构成。所有关系均作为共同概率空间中的边界排序谓词——点-点关系约归为误差函数，点-区间和区间-区间关系约归为由线性不等式诱导的多元高斯象限概率。接触关系（meets/starts/finishes/equals）通过容忍带获得正测度，单一容忍度下十三关系构成真划分，容忍度趋零时复原清晰Allen代数。构造演绎而非预设分类：先于、重叠、包含等粗谓词是叶关系的并集，其概率为叶概率之和，且该层级在区间坍缩为点时保持（十三→五→三）。每个关系进一步分解为符合CIDOC CRM精神的、考虑相关性的时间原语。代数具有尺度不变性，可区分"shortly before"等渐变表达与接触关系。

Result: 所得的概率Allen代数具备生成性和完备性，成功将不确定时间关系概率化。通过容忍带为接触关系赋予正测度，在单一容忍度下十三关系成为真划分，容忍度趋零时精确还原经典Allen代数。分类层级（先于、重叠、包含等粗谓词由叶并集构成）被保真保留，并在区间坍缩为点时依次约简为五种、三种关系。该代数尺度不变，能区分渐变表达与接触关系。全部结果经过蒙特卡洛验证，并已作为开源、经过测试的Python包发布。

Conclusion: 概率Allen代数以概率方式完整扩展了Allen区间代数，将关系概率从经验性打分推广为基于区间边界分布（高斯与截断高斯）的生成式推导。该构造不仅自然演绎出Allen的分类层级和坍缩约简，还通过容忍带统一了接触关系与渐变表达，且具备尺度不变性。它适用于语言、感知、数据库及不确定历史等场景，并以经过验证的开源Python实现支持实际应用。

Abstract: Allen's interval algebra is a qualitative calculus for temporal relations, but its thirteen base relations are crisp predicates over exact interval boundaries. This is inadequate for temporal information from language, perception, databases, or uncertain histories, where times, durations, and boundaries are uncertain and expressions such as "just before" or "roughly during" have graded meaning. We develop the probabilistic Allen algebra (PAA): a generative and complete extension in which relation probabilities are derived from distributions over interval boundaries rather than assigned as scores. Time points are Gaussian; intervals have Gaussian midpoints and truncated-Gaussian durations. Every relation is a boundary-ordering predicate in one common probability space: point-point relations reduce to error functions, and point-interval and interval-interval relations to multivariate Gaussian orthant probabilities induced by linear inequalities. Contact relations (meets, starts, finishes, equals) receive positive measure through a tolerance band, and under a single tolerance the thirteen relations form a true partition that recovers crisp Allen as the tolerance vanishes. The construction derives Allen's taxonomy rather than positing it: coarse predicates such as precedence, overlap, and containment are unions of leaves whose probabilities are leaf sums, and this hierarchy is preserved as intervals collapse to points and thirteen relations reduce to five and then three. Each relation further decomposes into correlation-aware temporal primitives in the spirit of CIDOC CRM. The algebra is scale-invariant and separates graded expressions such as "shortly before" from contact relations. All results are Monte-Carlo validated and shipped as an open, tested Python package.

</details>


### [258] [Ownership in AI-Assisted Everyday Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20658)
*Megan Wei,Melanie Subbiah,Audrey Lee,Annya Dahmani,Dave Edwards,Helen Edwards,Ellie Pavlick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过探索性定性调查，考察人们在使用AI完成工作时的"感知所有权"（felt ownership）。研究发现，所有权取决于协作过程：仅审批AI建议会使人"弃权"，而主导、迭代或改写则能保留所有权。拥有项目愿景（即使不亲自执行）也能带来高所有权，而丧失个人声音和对输出缺乏理解则会侵蚀所有权。是否愿意披露AI使用往往与实际自豪感或所有权脱钩，主要受社区规范和对功劳被抹去的担忧影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI逐渐融入日常任务，我们需要审视当机器参与生产时，人们对工作的所有权和贡献感会发生什么变化。这一问题是AI对人类能动性（agency）和作者身份（authorship）影响的核心关切。

Method: 采用探索性定性调查（exploratory qualitative survey）方法。参与者被要求描述两件近期、自选的用AI完成的任务：一件感觉属于自己的，一件感觉不属于自己的，以此对比分析影响感知所有权的因素。

Result: 研究发现四点：(1) 所有权取决于协作过程，仅审批建议会导致弃权，而主导、迭代、改写则保留所有权；(2) 即使在无法独立完成的执行情境下，若拥有项目愿景仍能获得高所有权；(3) 丧失个人声音与对输出缺乏理解会侵蚀所有权；(4) 披露AI使用的意愿与实际自豪/所有权脱钩，而由社区规范和害怕功劳被抹去所塑造。

Conclusion: 基于这些发现，作者提出若干研究方向的建议，旨在促进AI发展以支持人们对其自身生活的作者身份（authorship）感受。

Abstract: When does work done with AI still feel like ours? As AI becomes woven into everyday tasks, we must examine what happens to our sense of ownership and contribution when a machine shares in producing what we make. We report an exploratory qualitative survey in which participants were asked to describe two recent, self-selected tasks completed with AI: one that felt like their own and one that did not. We find that felt ownership depends on the process of collaboration: people disown work when they merely approve AI's suggestions, but retain ownership when they lead, iterate, or rewrite. Ownership can also extend to settings where people own the vision for a project but not the execution; respondents reported high ownership on tasks they could not have completed without AI. Loss of personal voice and a lack of comprehension of the output both erode ownership. Finally, willingness to disclose AI use is often decoupled from actual pride or ownership, and instead shaped by community norms and fear of credit erasure. We propose several research directions as a result of these findings to promote AI development that supports people's sense of authorship over their own lives.

</details>


### [259] [Deep Noir: Autonomous Steering Discovery via Architectural Chronometry in Transformer Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20722)
*Frank E. Bobe,Gregory D. Vetaw,Darshan W. Bryner,Matthew G. Cook,Jose L. Salas-Vernis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Deep Noir框架，利用Logit Lens收敛性和因果层注意力归因，自动发现最优激活引导（steering）参数，无需人工选择干预位置和强度。在1B级模型上垃圾邮件任务提升16.7个百分点，7-9B级模型上提升21-42个百分点，SST-2情感任务零代码改动提升13.1个百分点，并揭示引导会带来可预测的提示注入攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导（activation steering）能在推理时修改LLM行为，但当前确定"在哪个位置引导"以及"引导多强"仍依赖人工手动调参，缺乏自动化发现机制。作者旨在摆脱手工干预，实现引导参数的自发现。

Method: 结合两种机制：(1) Logit Lens收敛性用于定位干预层；(2) 因果层注意力归因（causal head-level attribution）用于确定具体注意力头及引导强度。框架在三组规模（1B×3、2-3B×2、7-9B×4）共9款模型上自动发现引导参数，并且无需修改代码即可跨任务迁移。

Result: 1B垃圾邮件任务提升16.7个百分点（标准差4.7，39次运行）；7-9B四架构上提升增至21-42个百分点；SST-2情感任务提升13.1个百分点（零代码改动）。在情感任务上，RepE无head masking时无法超越基线，而Deep Noir对所有模型带来显著提升（p<0.01）。此外发现引导会形成可预测的提示注入攻击面，其脆弱性随引导强度单调增加。

Conclusion: 机制层面的可解释基础（Logit Lens与因果归因）能够自动化发现跨任务、跨架构泛化的干预点，显著优于人工及RepE基线；同时需警惕引导带来的提示注入安全风险，对部署引导分类器的智能体系统尤为关键。

Abstract: Activation steering modifies LLM behavior at inference time, but identifying where and how strongly to steer remains manual. We introduce Deep Noir, a framework that uses Logit Lens convergence and causal head-level attribution to autonomously discover optimal steering parameters. Across three scales (1B x 3, 2-3B x 2, and 7-9B x 4), our engine achieves 16.7 percentage-point improvement on spam at 1B (standard deviation 4.7; 39 runs), with gains increasing to 21 to 42 percentage points at 7-9B across four architectures. On SST-2 sentiment, it achieves a 13.1 percentage-point improvement with zero code changes. Mechanistic grounding enables automated discovery of intervention points that generalize across tasks and architectures. On sentiment, RepE without head masking fails to improve over baseline, while Deep Noir improves all models (p less than 0.01). We further show that steering creates a predictable prompt-injection attack surface whose vulnerability increases monotonically with steering magnitude. This finding is relevant to agent systems deploying steered classifiers.

</details>


### [260] [Q&A on Any Spreadsheet Requires Interpreting Its Grid Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20732)
*Zofia Smoleń*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于语义单元角色标注的电子表格分块框架，将二维电子表格切分为可解释的文本块以提升LLM驱动RAG系统的可解释性和答案生成质量。该框架虽优于现有最先进方法，却遭遇硬性天花板：分类模型受限于有限预定义类别，无法完美捕捉电子表格中连续关系与无限单元角色所构成的结构复杂性。作者由此主张领域应超越离散单元分类，发展降维技术将二维非结构化电子表格直接压平为一维非结构化文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子表格在LLM驱动的RAG系统中的分块处理存在瓶颈。二维电子表格具有连续关系和无限潜在单元角色，而传统分类模型（即便有人类级标注）受限于有限预定义类别，无法充分捕捉这种结构细节，从而制约了解释性分块与后续答案生成的效果。

Method: 提出"语义单元角色标注"框架，为电子表格中的每个单元赋予语义角色并将其组织为可解释的分块，从而以丰富上下文而非提升检索准确率的方式辅助RAG系统生成答案。研究中对比了该框架与最先进方法，并验证其性能上限。

Result: 该框架在性能上超越了最先进的现有方法，但同时暴露了明显的性能天花板。实验表明，即便采用人类级别的单元角色标注，分类模型仍无法完全表征电子表格的内在二维结构与连续关系。这验证了离散单元分类方法的固有限制。

Conclusion: 解决电子表格到LLM之间的瓶颈不能依赖离散单元分类，而需发展维度约简技术，将二维非结构化电子表格直接压平为一维非结构化文本。由此生成的文本块更易于下游RAG系统解释和生成答案，为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: Semantic cell annotation improves chunking interpretability for spreadsheets in LLM-driven RAG systems, aiding answer generation through enriched context rather than improved retrieval accuracy. We propose a novel framework of splitting any spreadsheet into interpretable chunks using cell role annotation. Our framework beats the state of the art, yet it faces a hard ceiling. Spreadsheets are fundamentally two-dimensional unstructured data with continuous relationships and infinite potential cell roles. Because classification models are restricted to finite, pre-defined classes, they cannot perfectly capture this structural nuance, even with human-level annotation. We show that addressing the spreadsheet-to-LLM bottleneck requires moving beyond discrete cell classification. Instead, the field must develop dimensionality-reduction techniques to directly flatten 2D unstructured spreadsheets into 1D unstructured text. Text chunks would be easier for downstream RAG to interpret and generate from.

</details>


### [261] [An Empirical Study of Harness Design for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20804)
*Run-Ze Fan,Zihao Zhang,Simin Ma,Yebowen Hu,Shouju Wang,Kaiqiang Song,Fei Liu,Hamed Zamani,Xiaoyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文不再将编码工具链（coding harness）视为整体系统，而是将其拆解为规划、动作空间与上下文管理三个组件，在四个模型、SWE-Bench Verified 与 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上通过176组匹配设置进行组件级对比。结果表明：上下文管理在上下文预算收紧时价值提升，主要通过防止上下文溢出失效；先规则后LLM的分级省略是最强的上下文管理策略，而可恢复省略内容收效甚微；规划从弱模型的准确率支撑转变为强模型的成本节约工具；预定义工具利于bash能力弱的模型，而bash能力强的模型仅用bash接口即可达到更低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常把编码工具链当作单一整体进行评估，导致难以判断其中各个组件对自主编码智能体表现的实际贡献。作者希望打破这种黑箱式评估，实现组件层面的对照，从而为面向模型特性与预算约束的工具链设计提供依据。

Method: 作者构建了一个执行循环固定的轻量级编码工具链，仅变化三个组件——规划、动作空间与上下文管理。在四个模型上，对SWE-Bench Verified与Terminal-Bench 2.1进行评估，共覆盖176种匹配设置，包括五种上下文管理策略、四种上下文窗口预算，以及对规划和动作空间的目标消融实验，并辅以轨迹级分析。

Result: 实验结果给出四项核心发现：(1) 上下文预算是上下文管理价值的关键调节变量，预算越紧越有价值，收益主要来自防止上下文溢出导致的失败；(2) 先规则省略后LLM摘要的分级策略整体效率最高，而把省略内容做成可恢复状态增加的机制很少被模型使用且无准确率提升；(3) 规划的作用随模型能力变化，对弱模型是准确率支架，对强模型则转化为成本节省；(4) 预定义工具对bash能力弱的模型提升性能，而bash能力强的模型仅用bash接口即可高效运行并显著降低成本，尤其在命令行密集型任务上。轨迹级分析进一步解释：上下文管理延长执行轨迹但不显著改变行为，规划改变轨迹停止位置，动作空间改变写代码的粒度。

Conclusion: 这些发现为面向不同模型能力与上下文预算的工具链设计提供了指导，并建立了一个模块化框架，可用于未来对工具链各组件的评估与比较。

Abstract: Coding harnesses shape how autonomous coding agents translate model capabilities into long-horizon software-engineering performance, yet existing work typically evaluates harnesses as monolithic systems, leaving the effectiveness of individual components unclear. To enable component-level comparisons, we study this question with a lightweight coding harness whose execution loop is fixed while three components are varied: planning, action space, and context management. Across four models evaluated on SWE-Bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2.1, we evaluate 176 matched settings spanning five context-management strategies, four context-window budgets, and targeted ablations of planning and action space. We find that: (1) Context management becomes increasingly valuable as the context-window budget tightens, with most of its benefit coming from preventing context-overflow failures. (2) Staging rule-based elision before LLM-based summarization provides the strongest overall efficiency among the context-management strategies, whereas making elided content recoverable adds machinery that models rarely use and yields no accuracy gain. (3) Planning shifts from an accuracy scaffold for weaker models to a cost saver for stronger models, with little change in accuracy. (4) Predefined tools improve performance for models with weaker bash proficiency, whereas bash-capable models can operate effectively with a bash-only interface and achieve substantially lower cost, especially on command-line-centric tasks. Trajectory-level analysis explains these effects: context management extends execution trajectories without substantially altering agent behavior, planning changes where trajectories stop, and the action space changes the granularity at which code is written. These findings inform model- and budget-aware harness design and provide a modular framework for evaluating future harness components.

</details>


### [262] [RAFT: A Stateful Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Troubleshooting Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20754)
*Mingxuan Zhang,Xiaowen Wang,Anupma Sharan,Zhengyi Chen,Chenyu Diana Zhang,Shanshan Yang,Chittibabu Pacharu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAFT，一种面向多阶段、有状态技术支持案例检索的RAG框架，将历史案例抽象为时间线条目链并在条目级别检索，返回匹配状态锚定的父案例轨迹；实验显示在各进展阶段优于vanilla RAG和GraphRAG基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统将支持案例视为静态文档，忽略其多阶段、有状态的特性，导致故障排查代理难以从相似历史案例中检索到可操作的指导。

Method: 将每个已关闭历史案例抽象为有向的时间线条目链，在条目级别进行检索，使匹配中间状态的案例被召回，并返回以匹配状态为锚点的父案例轨迹；可选地通过可配置的相似性表示构建案例级图以连接案例。评估直接针对检索层，无需生产部署。使用微软Windows Server文档合成的基准数据及带人工重复标签的Apache Jira真实问题构建测试集。

Result: RAFT在案例进展的每个阶段均提升了Case Hit指标，相对最强基线取得统计显著增益；Jira结果提供了优势可迁移至真实案例历史的方向性证据。

Conclusion: 状态感知的条目级检索比静态文档检索更适用于多阶段故障排查，且可直接评估检索层降低部署成本；同时发布了基准、实现和Jira评估集。

Abstract: Effective troubleshooting agents in enterprise customer support depend on retrieving actionable guidance from similar historical cases, yet existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems treat support cases as static documents and overlook their multi-stage, stateful nature. We introduce RAFT (Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Troubleshooting Agents), a stateful RAG framework that abstracts each closed historical case into a directed chain of timeline entries and retrieves at the entry level, surfacing cases whose intermediate states match the active case and returning the parent-case trajectory anchored at the matched state; an optional case-level graph links cases through a configurable similarity representation. We evaluate this retrieval layer directly, which, unlike evaluating a full agent system, requires no production deployment. Because public multi-stage troubleshooting data is extremely rare, we pair a synthetic benchmark built from Microsoft Learn Windows Server documentation with real Apache Jira issues carrying human-created duplicate labels. RAFT improves Case Hit over vanilla RAG and GraphRAG baselines at every stage of case progress, with statistically significant gains over the strongest baseline; the Jira results provide directional evidence that the advantage transfers to real case histories. We release our benchmark, implementation, and the Apache Jira evaluation set.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [From Models to Systems: A Comprehensive Survey of Efficient Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19445)
*Pan Wang,Siwei Song,Hui Ji,Siqi Cao,Heng Yu,Zhijian Liu,Huanrui Yang,Yingyan Celine Lin,Beidi Chen,Mohit Bansal,Xiaoming Liu,Pengfei Zhou,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Tianlong Chen,Jingtong Hu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文献是一篇关于高效多模态学习（EML）的系统性综述，首次提出了结构化的"模型-算法-系统"三层分类体系，从超过300篇重要工作中提炼见解，深入剖析了跨层协同设计如何影响"效率-效用-隐私"的基本权衡，并通过多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的案例研究梳理了该领域从初始结构调整到现代全栈资源编排的演进轨迹，最终提出效率应作为模型内在涌现属性而非事后约束的范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型的快速发展带来了计算、内存和部署方面的严峻瓶颈，催生了高效多模态学习（EML）这一关键研究方向。尽管相关进展密集，但学术界对效率在学习栈中"是什么、如何体现、体现在何处"缺乏统一理解，现有知识呈现碎片化状态，亟需一个结构化框架来系统化梳理该领域。

Method: 作者引入了首个结构化的"模型到系统"分类体系，将EML划分为三个层级：模型层（解决架构简洁性/parsimony）、算法层（解决执行精炼/refinement）、系统层（解决硬件感知编排/orchestration）。在纯分类综述之外，作者还提供了跨层垂直协同的方法论综合，并通过MLLMs的整合性案例研究、应用特定域优化蓝图及开放挑战讨论来支撑分析。

Result: 该综述提炼了超过300篇代表性工作的核心见解，建立了三层层级分类，阐明了跨层协同设计对"效率-效用-隐私"权衡的贡献，基于MLLMs案例展示了从结构调整到全栈资源编排的演化路径，并为不同应用领域提供了优化蓝图，同时预测了向自调节智能的范式转移。

Conclusion: 本文为多模态系统建立了结构化框架，目标是构建不仅高性能、强泛化，而且原生高效、可广泛部署的系统。作者指出效率应成为模型基础设计的内在涌现属性而非事后约束，并提出了定义EML研究未来轨迹的开放挑战与方向。该综述有持续更新的在线版本（GitHub链接）。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of multimodal models has surfaced formidable bottlenecks in computation, memory, and deployment, catalyzing the rise of Efficient Multimodal Learning (EML) as a pivotal research frontier. Despite intensive progress, a cohesive understanding of what, how, and where efficiency is manifested across the learning stack remains fragmented. This survey systematizes the EML landscape by introducing the first structured, model-to-system taxonomy. We distill insights from over 300 seminal works into three hierarchical levels--model, algorithm, and system--addressing architectural parsimony, execution refinement, and hardware-aware orchestration, respectively. Moving beyond a purely categorical review, we offer a methodological synthesis of the vertical synergies between these layers, elucidating how cross-layer co-design contributes to the fundamental "Efficiency-Utility-Privacy" trade-off. Through an integrative case study of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), we trace the field's evolutionary trajectory from initial structural adjustments to modern full-stack resource orchestration. Furthermore, we provide a holistic discussion and application-specific optimization blueprints for diverse domains and posit a paradigm shift toward self-regulating intelligence, where efficiency is an intrinsic, emergent property of the model's fundamental design rather than a post-hoc constraint. Finally, we present open challenges and future directions that will define the trajectory of EML research. This survey establishes a structured framework for multimodal systems that are not only high-performing and generalizable but natively efficient and ready for ubiquitous deployment. A continuously updated version is available at https://github.com/pwang322/Efficient-Multimodal-Learning-Survey.

</details>


### [264] [Trigger Timing, Deadline Readiness, and Event-Aligned Accounting for Dynamic Ad Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19899)
*Prashant Chaudhary,Kapil Khandelwal*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文针对动态广告插入（DAI）比较中容易混淆触发、触达、就绪、播放、计费与测量等事件的问题，提出一种分离这些事件的分析框架：观测事件账本（observed-event ledger）、候选不变的参考截止时间以及pod级贡献核算。作者通过离线合成实验（45,000个共享脚本、18种条件）验证了该框架，发现播放头摘要（playhead summaries）在非单调混合场景下会严重误分类触达事件，但在此网格中不会反转贡献对比；而将“截止时间前保持有效”替换为“截止前完成”会在9个信息性条件中的3个反转对比。整个研究仅使用合成证据，不排序任何服务端方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态广告插入（DAI）比较中，即使算术核算在数学上正确，触发、触达、就绪、播放、计费与测量等不同事件仍常被混为一谈，导致对比结论失真。作者旨在建立一套能够分离这些事件、使"贡献会计"在候选分配前就预先固定的分析框架，从而让DAI策略比较不受事件定义混淆的干扰。

Method: 作者提出三类核心机制：(1) 观测事件账本，将触发、触达、就绪、播放、计费等事件在时间上分离记录；(2) 候选不变的参考截止时间，在候选分配前固定，并用于判定"可达的转移状态是否仍然有效"而非"准备是否更早完成"；(3) pod级贡献核算。此外引入"受限单调播放表示"来界定媒体位置摘要（media-position summaries）何时足以反映状态。实验采用离线合成研究，在18种条件（9种短生存期用于就绪比较、9种长生存期作为分析对照）下运行45,000个共享脚本，比较两种触发策略，并设计了针对截止时间定义与就绪评分时点的对照测试。

Result: 播放头摘要（playhead summaries）在非单调混合条件下会实质性地误分类触达（reach）事件；但在本实验网格中，两种触发策略的贡献对比并未反转，因为共享设计下部分误差相互抵消。将"截止时间有效（validity）"替换为"截止前完成（completion）"后，在9个信息性条件中的3个（全部位于三种延迟设置之一）反转了对比结果。在真实观众到达时点而非参考截止时间打分就绪情况，会改变暂停路径的就绪读数，但不会改变任一贡献符号。当记录缺失时，保守界仍无法消除不确定性。研究完全基于合成证据，未使用商业遥测数据，且不评价服务端或服务端引导插入方案的优劣。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明：DAI比较的结论高度依赖于事件定义的具体选择——尤其是"截止时间有效"与"截止前完成"的语义差异，以及就绪评分的时点选择（实际到达时 vs 参考截止时间）。既定的事件误分类机制（如播放头摘要对非单调路径的失效）在特定条件下足以改变策略对比的贡献符号，尽管在本实验网格中由误差抵消掩盖。因此，研究者必须在候选分配前固定参考截止时间与事件定义，使用观测事件账本分离各事件，才能获得稳健的贡献核算；当记录缺失时，保守界只能保留不确定性而不消除它。

Abstract: Dynamic ad insertion comparisons can conflate trigger, reach, readiness, playback, billability and measurement even when the accounting is arithmetically correct. We separate these events with an observed-event ledger, a candidate-invariant reference deadline and pod-level contribution accounting. The deadline rule is fixed before candidate assignment and tests whether an admissible transition state remains valid, not whether preparation merely finished earlier. A restricted monotone-playback representation states when media-position summaries suffice; a counterexample shows why they fail over a wider path class. An offline synthetic study exercises the definitions over nine short-lifetime conditions informative for the readiness comparison and nine long-lifetime conditions serving as analytic controls. Across 45,000 shared scripts, two trigger policies share imposed playback paths, latency draws and hypothetical value and cost coefficients. Playhead summaries substantially misclassify reach events in the nonmonotone mixtures, yet neither shortcut reverses the contribution contrast in this grid, because some errors cancel under the shared design. Replacing validity at the deadline with completion by the deadline reverses the comparison in three of the nine informative conditions, all at one of the three latency settings. Scoring readiness at actual viewer arrival rather than at the reference deadline shifts pause-path readiness but changes no contribution sign. These outcomes are consequences of the event definitions applied to established misclassification mechanisms. Conservative bounds retain uncertainty when records are missing, and the artifact records code, seeds, event histories and checking procedures. The evidence is synthetic, uses no commercial telemetry, and ranks neither server-side nor server-guided insertion.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [265] [Generative Query Suggestion via Intent Coverage and Query-Level Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19209)
*Xinpeng Liu,Lu Ma,Jiayi Qiao,Mengyu Zhou,Linglong Li,Xiaofeng Bian,Haonan Chen,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向生成式查询建议的意图驱动查询建议框架（Intent-Driven Query Suggestion Framework），采用双阶段优化。第一阶段通过意图对齐的监督微调数据和意图感知多样性奖励来优化意图覆盖；第二阶段通过查询级信用分配，将个体质量信号路由到对应查询词元，同时共享清单级多样性信号。在大规模生产数据集上的离线评估和在线A/B测试显示，该方法在点击率、查询质量和意图覆盖方面均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式查询建议旨在通过预测用户意图并推荐相关后续查询来提升用户参与度。其核心挑战在于生成既包含有用个体查询、又能覆盖不同意图的清单（slate），即需要在单条查询的有用性与整个清单的意图多样性之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出双阶段优化框架：(1) 意图感知多样性建模阶段——构建意图对齐的监督微调（SFT）数据，并设计意图感知多样性奖励（Intent-Aware Diversity Reward）来优化意图覆盖；(2) 查询级信用分配阶段——将个体质量信号路由到对应的查询词元，同时在整个清单上共享清单级多样性信号。

Result: 在大规模生产数据集上进行实验，包括在线A/B测试和离线评估，结果显示在点击率（CTR）、查询质量和意图覆盖三个方面均得到显著改进。

Conclusion: 实验证明该意图驱动框架能有效解决查询建议中个体有用性与清单多样性之间的权衡，通过双阶段优化同时提升查询质量、点击率和意图覆盖度，在真实生产环境中验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Generative query suggestion aims to enhance user engagement by anticipating user intents and recommending relevant follow-up queries. A central challenge is to generate slates whose individual queries are useful while the slate covers distinct intents. We propose an Intent-Driven Query Suggestion Framework with dual-stage optimization. First, intent-aware diversity modeling constructs intent-aligned supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data and uses an Intent-Aware Diversity Reward to optimize intent coverage. Second, query-level credit assignment routes individual quality signals to the corresponding query tokens while sharing a slate-level diversity signal across the slate. Experiments on a large-scale production dataset, including online A/B testing and offline evaluation, show improvements in click-through rate, query quality, and intent coverage.

</details>


### [266] [Layer-wise Curriculum Learning for Efficient LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19213)
*Donggeon Lee,Dooyeon Na,Seungmin Oh,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于高效大语言模型（LLM）压缩的逐层课程学习方法。该方法将教师模型到学生模型的知识迁移组织为课程学习流程，从简单优化任务逐步过渡到困难任务；通过将模型划分为若干层段实现计算高效的知识迁移，并基于累积误差的理论分析加速收敛、稳定迁移过程。同时引入特征缓存与多线程策略解决跨层特征错位问题，最大化GPU利用率。实验表明该方法在BERT和GPT-2上达到最先进性能，同时将GPU内存与训练时间减少超过50%；在同样训练时长下，其在LLaMA系列和Qwen模型上优于其他剪枝方法，且内存占用更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM压缩（剪枝/蒸馏）面临两大挑战：一是教师到学生的大规模知识迁移计算开销巨大、训练时间长；二是深层模型存在累积误差现象（前面层的误差逐层放大），导致直接进行整体迁移时收敛慢、训练不稳定。现有压缩方法缺乏对错误逐层传播的机理分析，也难以在有限GPU资源下实现高效、稳定的压缩。

Method: 1) 层段划分：将整个LLM按层划分为多个区段，以区段为单位进行知识迁移，降低单步计算复杂度；2) 课程学习调度：按照从浅层到深层（由易到难）的顺序组织优化任务，基于对累积误差现象的理论分析设计训练顺序，使收敛加速并稳定迁移；3) 特征缓存与多线程：针对层间特征错位问题设计特征缓存机制，结合多线程调度，最大化GPU利用率，减少等待与内存占用。

Result: 在多个数据集上，该方法达到state-of-the-art压缩性能；在BERT和GPT-2上GPU内存与训练时间均减少50%以上；在相同训练时长约束下，该方法在LLaMA系列与Qwen模型上优于其他剪枝方法，且GPU内存占用更低，兼顾了压缩质量与计算效率。

Conclusion: 逐层课程学习结合理论化的累积误差分析，能够实现高效稳定的LLM知识迁移压缩。该方法在精度上达到最优的同时显著降低训练成本（内存与时间），为大型语言模型的高效压缩提供了可行方案，且对LLaMA、Qwen等现代大模型同样适用。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce layer-wise curriculum learning for efficient LLM compression. The proposed method facilitates the knowledge transfer from the teacher model to the student model, utilizing a curriculum learning approach that begins with easier optimization tasks and progressively tackles harder ones. In order to adopt the layer-wise learning in LLM compression, we partition the whole model into multiple segments consisting of layers, thereby enabling more computationally efficient knowledge transfer for LLMs. Based on our theoretical analysis of cumulative error phenomenon, layer-wise curriculum learning accelerates convergence while stabilizing the knowledge transfer process. In addition, we present a feature caching method with a multi-threading strategy to efficiently address feature misalignment across layers, maximizing GPU utilization. Consequently, our method exhibits advanced model compression performance, as well as high computational efficiency in terms of minimized memory usage and short training hours. Experiments on multiple datasets show that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing GPU memory usage and training hours by more than 50\% on BERT and GPT-2. Moreover, it outperforms the other pruning methods on LLaMA-family and Qwen models under the same training hours, with a lower GPU memory footprint.

</details>


### [267] [Block Parallelism For Efficient Distributed Long-Context Diffusion Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19242)
*Tarun Suresh,Pranshu Chaturvedi,Hangoo Kang,Parth Shroff,Ishan S. Khare,Hermann Kumbong,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出块并行（BP）及上下文分片块并行（CSBP）两种新的分布式并行维度，用于扩展块扩散语言模型（BDLM）的长上下文训练。CSBP将共享干净序列分片、将损坏块计算分配给各rank，保持损坏K/V和梯度本地化、避免干净前缀复制，在16块H200 GPU上（256K上下文）相比最优基线提升1.18-1.45x SFT吞吐量、1.27-1.33x自回归模型转BDLM吞吐量，且在8块H100上加速DFlash2推测解码器训练2.48x（512K）和7.59x（1M）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BDLM虽然将跨块自回归依赖与块内并行去噪结合，但长上下文训练受限于分布式注意力通信和激活内存开销。现有的上下文并行（CP）按位置分片"干净+损坏"联合序列，需同时通信共享的干净K/V、块特定的损坏K/V及其梯度，导致通信和内存瓶颈。作者观察到BDLM目标函数对目标块是可分离的，因此可设计新的并行策略来克服CP的局限。

Method: 提出两个新的并行维度：(1) 块并行（BP）——一种新的分布式并行维度，将每个损坏块的计算分配给一个rank；(2) 上下文分片块并行（CSBP）——为扩展BP到长上下文，同时将共享的干净序列在这些rank间分片。CSBP的关键特性：保持损坏K/V和梯度本地化、避免复制干净前缀、保留BDLM训练语义。并公开了Turbo-dLLM代码实现。

Result: 在16块H200 GPU、256K上下文下，CSBP相对最优基线在SFT场景提升吞吐量1.18-1.45x，在自回归模型转BDLM场景提升1.27-1.33x，同时峰值HBM持平或降低；全模型加速在512K达1.61x。在8块H100上，CSBP加速DFlash2推测解码器训练2.48x（512K）和7.59x（1M）。在匹配的12小时DiffusionGemma 26B-A4B SFT实验中，CSBP在每个训练检查点均在SWE-bench Verified和Terminal-Bench Lite上取得更高通过率。

Conclusion: CSBP通过利用BDLM目标对目标块的分离性，将损坏块计算和共享上下文分片分配到各rank，显著减少长上下文训练中的通信与激活内存开销，在多种硬件（H200/H100）和多种任务（SFT、自回归转BDLM、推测解码器训练）上均取得一致的吞吐量提升，并展现出更好的收敛表现。该方法为BLLM（block diffusion/LM）的长上下文高效训练提供了新的并行策略，代码已开源。

Abstract: Block diffusion language models (BDLMs) combine autoregressive dependencies across blocks with parallel denoising within blocks, but long-context training is constrained by distributed attention communication and activation memory. Conventional context parallelism (CP) shards the combined clean-plus-corrupted sequence by position, communicating shared clean K/V together with block-specific corrupted K/V and their gradients. We observe that the BDLM objective separates over target blocks. We introduce block parallelism (BP), a new distributed parallelism dimension that assigns each corrupted-block computation to one rank. To scale BP to long contexts, we introduce context-sharded block parallelism (CSBP), which also shards the shared clean sequence across those ranks. CSBP keeps corrupted K/V and gradients local, avoids replicated clean prefixes, and preserves BDLM training semantics. On 16 H200 GPUs at 256K context, CSBP improves throughput over the best baseline by 1.18-1.45x for supervised fine-tuning and 1.27-1.33x for conversion of autoregressive models to BDLMs, while matching or reducing peak HBM. Full-model speedup reaches 1.61x at 512K. On eight H100 GPUs, CSBP accelerates DFlash2 speculative-decoder training by 2.48x at 512K and 7.59x at 1M. In matched 12-hour DiffusionGemma 26B-A4B SFT runs, CSBP achieves higher pass rates at every trained checkpoint on SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench Lite. Code: https://github.com/ScalingIntelligence/Turbo-dLLM

</details>


### [268] [Randomized SVD Approximations for Spectral Co-Clustering of Word-Document Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19243)
*Fateme Mazdarani,Carlos Toxtli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种用于二分文本数据归一化谱共聚类的随机近似方法：基于随机投影的随机SVD，以及结合部分SVD与逐元素随机采样的方法。两种方法相比完整SVD基线均降低运行时间，但效果取决于矩阵稀疏性：随机投影法在各设定下更可靠，而采样法对稠密矩阵更有效，对稀疏文本数据收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱共聚类是发现词-文档矩阵潜在结构的有效工具，但其依赖于奇异值分解（SVD），在数据维度较高时计算成本高昂，限制了其在大规模高维数据上的应用。

Method: 提出两种随机近似策略用于归一化谱共聚类：第一种通过随机投影实现随机SVD；第二种将部分SVD与逐元素随机采样相结合。两种方法均允许文档簇与词簇数量不同，并在真实世界与合成数据集上进行验证。

Result: 在真实与合成数据集上，两种方法相较完整SVD基线均缩短了运行时间，但性能依赖于矩阵稀疏程度：随机投影法在测试设定中更可靠，而采样法仅在稠密矩阵上作用明显，对已稀疏的文本数据改善有限。

Conclusion: 随机近似谱共聚类方法的选择应依据数据的底层结构（稀疏度）而定，为高维稀疏文本数据提供了一种兼顾精度与效率的实用策略。

Abstract: Spectral co-clustering is a useful tool for discovering latent structure in word-document matrices, but its reliance on singular value decomposition (SVD) can make standard formulations expensive on high-dimensional data. This paper presents two randomized approximations for normalized spectral co-clustering of bipartite text data when the numbers of document and word clusters may differ. The first method uses randomized SVD through random projection, while the second combines partial SVD with element-wise random sampling. Across real-world and synthetic datasets, both methods reduce runtime relative to the full-SVD baseline, but their behavior depends on matrix sparsity. The random projection method is the more reliable approximation across the tested settings, whereas the sampling-based method is most useful on denser matrices and provides limited benefit on already sparse text data. These results show that randomized approximations for spectral co-clustering should be selected according to the underlying structure of the data.

</details>


### [269] [Radio-Frequency Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19279)
*Zhihui Gao,Shi-Yuan Ma,Yiran Chen,Dirk Englund,Tingjun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出射频卷积神经网络（RF-CNN），通过重新利用无线设备中已有的频率混频器来执行CNN推理。混频器在时域上相乘信号，天然在频域执行卷积，从而将多通道卷积映射到频率音调上，由无源混频器单次完成。实验表明RF-CNN可运行高达2640万参数、9层的深度CNN，性能接近全精度，且每乘累加运算能耗低至0.72飞焦耳，比额外数字处理器低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备（智能手机、可穿戴、无人机）直接运行AI模型虽有低延迟、可扩展和隐私优势，但缺乏现代神经网络所需的计算能力。现有边缘加速器需额外增加计算硬件，进一步加剧设备在尺寸、重量、功耗和成本（SWaP-C）上的约束。作者旨在寻找不额外增加硬件的替代方案。

Method: 利用每个无线射频中已配备的混频器：混频器在时域执行信号相乘，这天然对应频域的卷积运算。作者将多通道卷积映射到不同频率音调上，由无源混频器一次性执行，实现RF-CNN。权重通过空中无线传输到达，模拟硬件与通信功能共享，因此边缘设备仅需在数据准备和读出阶段消耗能量。

Result: 实验证明RF-CNN能运行包含2640万参数、9层的深度CNN，涵盖无线信号分类、图像分类乃至可控图像生成任务，性能接近全精度水平。计算效率上，每乘累加运算能耗低至0.72飞焦耳，比在额外数字处理器上执行低两个数量级。

Conclusion: 已部署的无线基础设施（每台设备都已有的通信硬件）可以为数十亿已连接的设备带来高效、最先进的AI推理能力，无需为设备额外增加计算硬件，同时兼顾低延迟、可扩展性和数据隐私。

Abstract: Running artificial intelligence (AI) models directly on edge devices such as smartphones, wearables, and drones offers low latency, pervasive scalability, and data privacy, but these devices rarely carry the computing capability that modern neural networks demand. Edge accelerators have been developed in response, yet each adds computing hardware to devices already constrained in size, weight, power, and cost (SWaP-C). An alternative lies in what these devices already carry: the frequency mixer in every wireless radio multiplies signals in time, natively performing convolution in the frequency domain. Here we introduce radio-frequency convolutional neural networks (RF-CNNs), which repurpose existing communication hardware for CNN inference. Multi-channel convolutions are mapped onto frequency tones for a passive mixer to execute in a single pass. We experimentally demonstrate that RF-CNN runs deep CNNs up to 26.4 million parameters and nine layers from classification of wireless signals and images to controllable image generation, close to full-precision performance. Because the weights arrive over the air and the analog hardware is shared with communication, the edge device spends energy only on data preparation and readout-down to 0.72 femtojoules per multiply-accumulate, two orders of magnitude less than it would cost on an added digital processor. These results suggest that deployed wireless infrastructure can bring efficient, state-of-the-art AI inference to the billions of devices it already connects.

</details>


### [270] [SAGG: Sample-Adaptive Gradient Gating for Robust Multimodal Learning under Heterogeneous Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20302)
*Wentao Zhang,Yifan Zhu,Yutong Zhang,Wentao Mo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态学习中梯度平衡问题，证明在样本异质损坏模型下，所有批次级样本无关的线性估计器都会产生不可约偏差，并提出样本自适应的二元门控方法SAGG，可在高损坏场景下优于现有十种方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态梯度平衡方法用每个模态共享的标量调制编码器梯度，隐含假设整个训练批次的损坏是均匀的。但实际中损坏是样本异质的：同一小批次内不同样本可能损坏不同模态。作者想要解决这种批次级调制导致的不可约偏差问题。

Method: 首先通过理论证明，在异质损坏模型下，任何批次级样本无关的线性估计器（共享调制参数）对干净数据梯度存在不可约偏差；在自然的分布无关估计器类内，样本级的all-or-nothing门控是唯一无偏策略。据此提出SAGG（Sample-Adaptive Gradient Gating）：通过在线特征范数质量测试对每个样本做二元保留或丢弃决策，并加入截断机制控制方差。同时给出独立编码器架构的认证鲁棒半径。

Result: 理论证明了SAGG-based SGD以标准O(1/sqrt(T))速率收敛到干净损失的平稳点，且无损坏依赖的误差下限。在Kinetics-Sounds和UCF-101上，针对高斯噪声注入、部分模态缺失和自然贡献不平衡实验，SAGG一致优于十种现有方法，在高损坏率（批次级偏差最严重）场景下增益最大。

Conclusion: 样本级二元门控是应对异质损坏的独特无偏策略，SAGG通过在线质量测试+截断机制有效解决了批次级共享调制带来的偏差问题，在理论收敛性和实证性能上都优于现有方法，验证了从批次级标量调制转向样本级自适应决策的必要性。

Abstract: Multimodal gradient balancing methods modulate encoder gradients with a shared scalar per modality, implicitly assuming that corruption is uniform across the training batch. In practice, corruption is sample-heterogeneous: within a single mini-batch, different samples may have different modalities corrupted. We prove that under this heterogeneous corruption model, any batch-level sample-agnostic linear estimator with a shared modulation parameter incurs an irreducible bias with respect to the clean-data gradient, and that sample-level all-or-nothing gating is the unique unbiased strategy within a natural distribution-free estimator class. Motivated by this result, we propose Sample-Adaptive Gradient Gating (SAGG), which makes a binary retain-or-discard decision per sample via an online feature-norm quality test and incorporates a truncation mechanism for variance control. We prove that SAGG-based SGD converges at the standard O(1/sqrt(T)) rate to stationary points of the clean loss without a corruption-dependent error floor, and derive a certified robustness radius for the independent-encoder architecture that connects per-modality Lipschitz constants to the classification margin. Experiments on Kinetics-Sounds and UCF-101 under Gaussian noise injection, partial modality missing, and natural contribution imbalance show that SAGG consistently outperforms ten existing methods, with the largest gains in high-corruption regimes where batch-level bias is most severe.

</details>


### [271] [Learning-Induced Dynamical Transition in Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19288)
*Varun Vaidya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个非平衡动力学平均场理论（DMFT）框架，用以描述循环神经网络在学习过程中从混沌动力学向稳定任务相关动力学转变的过程。理论识别出关键反馈强度和依赖于学习率的关键时间点，并定量预测训练过程中的网络输出演化，与数值模拟吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解循环神经网络中学习如何重塑网络的底层动力学动态至关重要，因为这种从混沌到稳定的转变是任务执行的基础。然而，现有理论多关注已训练后的稳态行为，缺乏对训练过程中这一动态转变发生机制的理论描述。本文旨在填补这一空白，为学习诱导的动力学相变提供理论解释。

Method: 作者发展了一种非平衡动力学平均场理论（non-equilibrium DMFT）。核心思路是：将缓慢的、由反馈驱动的学习过程建模为有效反馈强度随时间演化，从而将网络从混沌推向稳定。通过推导贯穿学习全过程的双时间关联函数，定义DMFT解的分岔条件来确定临界反馈强度，并引入依赖于学习率的临界时间来刻画两种动力学状态的分界。

Result: 理论表明，该转变源于学习所构建的反馈结构对有效动力学景观的渐进性变形。从未训练状态出发，该理论能够定量预测训练期间网络输出的时间演化，并与数值模拟结果呈现定量一致。研究识别出关键反馈强度及相应的、依赖于学习率的临界时间，明确划分混沌与稳定两个动力学区域。

Conclusion: 该工作为循环神经网络学习过程中的混沌-稳定动力学转变提供了首个非平衡理论框架，揭示了学习诱导的反馈结构如何通过变形动力学景观驱动相变，为理解神经网络的训练动力学机制提供了理论工具，且具备定量预测能力。

Abstract: Learning in recurrent neural networks can fundamentally reshape their underlying dynamics, transforming initially chaotic activity into stable task-dependent behavior. We develop a non-equilibrium dynamical mean-field theory(DMFT) to describe this transition during learning. We show that a slow feedback-driven learning process generates an evolving effective feedback strength that drives the network through a transition from chaotic to stable dynamics defined by a bifurcation of the DMFT solution. By deriving the two-time correlation function throughout learning, we identify a critical feedback strength and a corresponding learning rate dependent critical time separating these regimes. The transition arises from the progressive deformation of an effective dynamical landscape by the growing learned feedback structure. Starting from the untrained state, the theory predicts the time evolution of the network output during training and shows quantitative agreement with numerical simulations.

</details>


### [272] [Personalized Federated Hierarchical Gaussian Processes for Privacy-Preserving Modeling of Heterogeneous Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19337)
*Xianjian Xie,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出pFedHGP，一种面向异构客户端的个性化联邦分层高斯过程模型，用于概率回归与分类任务。每个客户端的潜在函数分解为共享全局分量、保留全局核结构的个体偏差及灵活局部残差三部分，通过稀疏诱导变量近似与联邦变分推断实现原始数据不出本地，仅同步共享分量的低维统计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际场景中数据分布在多个异构客户端，传统联邦学习难以提供完整的概率预测分布，且现有方法往往忽视客户端间的相关性结构与个性化需求，难以在保护隐私的同时进行稳健的不确定性建模。

Method: 将每个客户端的潜在函数分解为三部分：共享全局分量、继承全局核结构的客户端特定偏差以及灵活的局部残差。采用稀疏诱导变量近似降低计算复杂度，通过联邦变分推断实现服务器仅同步共享分量的低维统计量，原始数据始终保留在本地。此外用瞬时线性混合模型视角将分层结构与多输出高斯过程建立联系。

Result: 在冲压吨位监测应用中仅用13.77%标注周期即实现完美的故障分类；在联邦空气质量建模中无需集中站点级时间序列即可恢复地理分区，验证了模型在分类与回归任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: pFedHGP在联邦框架下同时实现了概率输出、个性化建模与数据隐私保护，并通过与多输出高斯过程的联系为相关传感器场景提供了理论支撑，展现出在故障监测、环境建模等领域的实用价值。

Abstract: We present Personalized Federated Hierarchical Gaussian Processes (pFedHGP) for probabilistic regression and classification when data are distributed across heterogeneous clients. Each client's latent function decomposes into (i) a shared global component, (ii) a client-specific deviation that shares the global kernel structure, and (iii) a flexible local residual. Sparse inducing-variable approximations and federated variational inference keep raw data local while the server synchronizes only low-dimensional statistics for the shared component. Full predictive distributions support uncertainty-aware decisions. In application studies, pFedHGP attains perfect fault classification in press tonnage monitoring using 13.77% of labeled cycles and recovers geographic zones in federated air-quality modeling without centralizing station-level time series. An Instantaneous Linear Mixing Model viewpoint links the hierarchy to multi-output Gaussian processes for correlated sensors.

</details>


### [273] [How to Guide Your Language Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19356)
*Rohit Dilip,Tianrong Chen,Yuyang Wang,David Van Valen,Joshua Susskind,Miguel Angel Bautista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"探针引导"（probe guidance）的新方法，通过利用已有扩散模型的冻结内部状态来构建引导信号，无需推理时的额外前向传播即可提升流匹配模型的生成质量。该方法在连续扩散语言模型上刷新了无条件生成的最优性能，并在多项选择问答基准上持续改进1.7B扩散语言模型。此外，该工作揭示了自引导（autoguidance）背后的真实机制，指出弱模型必须来自训练中的低熵区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自引导方法虽能提升生成质量，但需要推理时额外的模型前向传播，计算开销大；同时其底层机制尚不清楚，缺乏可靠途径保证弱模型与强模型共享相似动力学。作者旨在设计一种无需额外前向传播、且能阐明自引导内在原理的引导方法。

Method: 该方法利用已有扩散模型（强模型）的冻结内部状态构造引导信号，工作原理与自引导类似，但通过探针（probe）将内部状态映射为引导方向，从而省去推理时的第二次前向传播。作者将其应用于连续扩散语言模型，并在传统自引导设定下（以弱检查点作为强模型）进行机制研究。

Result: 探针引导在连续扩散语言模型的无条件生成任务上取得了新的最优性能；当应用于1.7B扩散语言模型时，在多项选择问答基准上持续改善。机制研究表明，自引导中弱模型必须来自训练的低熵区域才能发挥作用。

Conclusion: 探针引导既为扩散语言模型提供了实用改进途径，又阐明了自引导的实际机制（此前对此理解不足），证明弱模型需取自训练低熵区域是自引导有效性的关键条件。

Abstract: We introduce a new method to guide flow matching models. Our approach, which we call probe guidance, uses the frozen internal states of an existing diffusion model to construct a guidance signal. This works using a similar principle as autoguidance, but eliminates the need for an additional forward pass at inference time and provides a reliable path to ensure that the weak and strong model share similar dynamics. We apply and benchmark this method on continuous diffusion language models, where probe guidance sets a new state-of-the-art performance on unconditional generation. When applied to a 1.7B diffusion language model, probe guidance consistently improves on multiple choice question answering benchmarks. Using our probes, we study the traditional autoguidance setting where the strong model is a weak checkpoint, and find that the weak model must come from a low-entropy region of training. These findings both provide a practical way to improve diffusion language models and shed light on the actual mechanism behind autoguidance, which is currently poorly understood.

</details>


### [274] [Smart Insole Human Activity Recognition for Continuous Monitoring in Elderly Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19359)
*Edwin Rios,Antony Garcia,Fengpei Yuan,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无线智能鞋垫平台与机器学习流程，利用足底压力（16个传感点）和六维IMU信号（三轴加速度与角速度）来识别坐、站、行走和不稳定行走四种活动状态。在15名健康成年人80Hz数据上，采用参与者独立的分层分组交叉验证，直方图梯度提升（HGB）在左右脚分别达到0.954和0.959的macro-F1，双侧传感达0.980，且1D-CNN未显著优于HGB，展示了低剖面足部传感对未见参与者的活动推断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人跌倒常常以活动能力、平衡能力和姿势转换的变化为先兆，因此需要一种可穿戴、低剖面且能实时识别活动状态的系统，以便进行活动监测和跌倒预防，为老年人护理提供依据。

Method: 每只鞋垫集成16个压力传感位点和包含三轴加速度与角速度的六维IMU；从15名健康成年人以80Hz采集数据并分割为重叠窗口。先以分层10折交叉验证筛选窗口长度与候选模型族，再用参与者独立的5折Stratified Group交叉验证（同一参与者的所有窗口仅属同一折）给出主要性能估计；对比了直方图梯度提升（HGB）与紧凑型1D-CNN。

Result: 在参与者独立验证协议下，HGB对左右脚分别取得0.954和0.959的macro-F1，采用双侧传感时达到0.980；用相同参与者独立折评估的1D-CNN并未显著优于HGB（p=0.0625）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，低剖面足部传感可从压力与IMU测量信号中推断训练中未见参与者的活动状态，为老年人护理中的活动监测与跌倒预防建立了基础。

Abstract: Falls in older adults are often preceded by changes in mobility, balance, and postural transitions. This paper presents a wireless smart insole platform and machine-learning workflow for recognizing sitting, standing, walking, and unstable walking from plantar-pressure and inertial signals. Each insole integrates 16 active pressure-sensing locations and a six-dimensional IMU stream consisting of tri-axial acceleration and angular velocity. Data were collected from 15 healthy adults at 80~Hz and segmented into overlapping windows. Window length and candidate model families were first screened with stratified 10-fold cross-validation; the primary performance estimate was then obtained with participant-independent 5-fold Stratified Group cross-validation, ensuring that all windows from a participant remained in a single fold. Under this protocol, Histogram-Based Gradient Boosting (HGB) achieved macro-F1 scores of 0.954 and 0.959 for the left and right feet, respectively, and 0.980 with bilateral sensing. A compact 1D-CNN evaluated with the same participant-independent folds did not significantly outperform HGB ($p=0.0625$). The results show that low-profile footwear sensing can infer activity state from pressure and IMU measurements for participants unseen during training, establishing a basis for activity monitoring and fall prevention in elderly care.

</details>


### [275] [Stiefel Attention: When the Geometry of Transformer Projection Matrices Dominates Optimizer Choice---and When It Does Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19363)
*Rubén Darío Guerrero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将注意力机制中的查询和键投影矩阵约束在Stiefel流形上，并用自定义的Riemannian Adam（每帧单一标量二阶矩、信任区域限步、极坐标回缩）优化。理论证明该更新是嵌入度量下的最陡下降、与梯度尺度无关、良态且精确O(d)-等变；并证明权重衰减在Stiefel流形上的Riemannian梯度恒为零，从而保护注意力几何不被模型其余部分的塌缩周期破坏。实验上，模算术grokking任务单次运行在20000轮保持97.0%验证准确率（基线61.1%）；CIFAR-10 patches上12对随机起点平均提升+8.98个百分点（t=60.6，12/12全胜），且差距随数据量扩大而非缩小。消融表明全部收益来自步长尺度的自由化，而非投影器或等变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力中的Q/K投影几乎总是用无几何约束的欧几里得优化器训练，其几何结构未被显式建模或保护。本文动机是：通过把Q/K限制在Stiefel流形上并用黎曼优化，既获得尺度无关、条件良好、精确等变的更新规则，又借助权重衰减的黎曼梯度恒为零这一机制，使学到的注意力几何能在模型其余部分经历的塌缩循环中存活，从而改善泛化与稳定性。

Method: 方法：(1) 将WQ,WK约束到Stiefel流形St(d,r)；(2) 采用Riemannian Adam：每帧只有一个标量二阶矩，用信任区域限制步长，用极坐标回缩(retraction)；(3) 五个命题分别证明该更新是嵌入度量下的最陡下降、与梯度尺度无关、良态、精确O(d)-等变，以及权重衰减在Stiefel流形上的Riemannian梯度恒为零（因W=WI_r位于法空间）；(4) 全部数值验证在float64下进行；(5) 通过固定步长与AdamW的对比、冻结帧、以及消融实验（去掉投影器或等变性）来归因收益；(6) 用"规范去除(gauge removal)方向的损失不变故无梯度"这一负结果排除该动机。

Result: 结果：(1) 模算术grokking上单次运行在第20000轮保持97.0%验证准确率，而基线仅61.1%（被视为机制证据而非效应量）；(2) CIFAR-10 patches上同一规则在12对配对随机起点中平均提升+8.98个百分点（t=60.6，12/12全胜），且差距随数据增多而扩大；(3) 机理层面：固定步长黎曼更新对梯度是1次的，因此每步位移比同形状AdamW矩阵少24–40倍，帧几乎不离开初始化，完全冻结帧仅损失0.28个百分点；(4) 消融表明全部收益归因于步长尺度自由化，投影器与等变性无可测量贡献；(5) 负结果：沿损失不变（规范）方向的更新不携带任何梯度，因此"移除规范"不能作为方法动机。

Conclusion: 结论：将Q/K投影限制在Stiefel流形并采用带信任区域限步、极坐标回缩的Riemannian Adam是有效且有理论依据的优化方案。其主要机制是步长尺度的自由化（而非投影器或等变性），并通过权重衰减黎曼梯度恒为零在流形上"免疫"地保留注意力几何，使其在其余模型的塌缩循环中存活，进而带来显著且随数据增强的准确率提升。此外，规范移除不能成为该方法的合理动机。

Abstract: The query and key projections $\WQ,\WK$ in attention are almost always trained by Euclidean optimizers with no constraint on their geometry. We constrain them to the Stiefel manifold and optimize them there with a Riemannian Adam that carries one scalar second moment per frame, caps its step by a trust region, and retracts polarly. Four propositions prove this update is steepest descent in the embedded metric, independent of gradient scale, well conditioned, and exactly $\mathrm{O}(d)$-equivariant, each certified numerically in \texttt{float64}. A fifth supplies the mechanism: weight decay has \emph{identically zero} Riemannian gradient on $\St(d,r)$, since $W = W I_r$ lies in the normal space, so the learned attention geometry survives the collapse cycles that decay drives through the rest of the model. On modular arithmetic grokking, a single run holds $97.0\%$ validation accuracy at epoch 20\,000 against the baseline's $61.1\%$---an unstable endpoint we report as evidence for the mechanism rather than as an effect size. On CIFAR-10 patches the same rule gains $\mathbf{+8.98}$\,pp over 12 paired starts ($t{=}60.6$, $12/12$), and the gap widens with data rather than eroding. The step rule earns this: a fixed-step Riemannian update is degree one in the gradient, so it moves $24$--$40\times$ less per step than an identically shaped AdamW matrix---its frames barely leave their initialization, and freezing them outright costs only $0.28$\,pp. An ablation credits the whole gain to making the step scale free, and nothing measurable to the projector or to equivariance. A negative result sharpens the account: gauge removal cannot motivate the method, because a direction along which the loss is invariant carries no gradient at all.

</details>


### [276] [Machine-Learning Assessment of the Predictive Value of Inflammatory Biomarkers for Cognitive Impairment in an Older Hispanic Adult Cohort](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19374)
*Antony Garcia,Gabrielle Britton,Alcibiades Villarreal,Diana Oviedo,Giselle Rangel,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在小型临床表格数据集（PARI-HD队列，n=165）上采用防泄漏的阈值似然伯努利/类别朴素贝叶斯（BNB/CNB）分类器预测认知障碍。在30次重复分层10折交叉验证中，人口学基线ROC-AUC为0.630±0.017，而I-309（CCL1）作为增量特征使AUC提升0.110，且通过多重检验校正后仍显著，被确定为最具可解释性的候选预测特征，有待外部验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型临床表格数据集中，深度学习往往不实用，集成模型难以检验，因此需要可解释的机器学习方法。作者同时强调一个关键陷阱：统计显著性不一定意味着预测效用，因此需要严格评估特征的增量预测价值。

Method: 采用防泄漏的阈值似然BNB/CNB分类器。每一步训练折内，将连续预测变量降维为基于卡方检验的监督状态，而收入通过类别似然进入模型。所有依赖数据的过程均在30次重复的分层10折交叉验证内完成，并对I-309特征进行固定分区DeLong检验及200个随机分区稳健性评估，使用Benjamini-Hochberg校正控制探索性多重检验。

Result: 人口学基线ROC-AUC为0.630±0.017；I-309（CCL1）为主导增量特征，使AUC提升0.110，配对DeLong检验在100%重复中p<0.05。预设主分析中固定分区DeLong p=0.0018，200随机分区中位p=0.0011。在18个候选标记中，I-309的BH校正q=0.032，且在85%随机分区中满足q<0.05，其余标记均无可信的增量预测价值。

Conclusion: 由于所拟合模型是可检验的阈值与类别条件概率表，结果将I-309/CCL1确认为表格化认知障碍预测的可解释候选特征，但仍需外部验证加以确认。

Abstract: Small clinical tabular datasets require interpretable machine learning because deep learning is often impractical and ensemble models can be difficult to inspect. A key pitfall is that statistical significance does not necessarily imply predictive utility. Using data from the Panama Aging Research Initiative--Health Disparities (PARI-HD) cohort (n=165), we implemented a leakage-safe threshold-likelihood Bernoulli/Categorical Naive Bayes (BNB/CNB) classifier. Within every training fold, each continuous predictor was reduced to a supervised chi-square-derived state, while income entered the model through a categorical likelihood. All data-dependent steps were performed within repeated stratified 10-fold cross-validation with 30 repeats. The demographic baseline achieved a ROC-AUC of 0.630 +/- 0.017. I-309 (CCL1) was the dominant incremental feature, increasing AUC by 0.110, with paired DeLong tests yielding p<0.05 in 100% of repeats. In the pre-specified primary analysis, I-309 produced a fixed-partition DeLong p=0.0018, with robustness assessed across 200 random partitions, where the median p-value was 0.0011. Within the exploratory family of 18 candidate markers, I-309 achieved a Benjamini-Hochberg-adjusted q=0.032 on the frozen partition and satisfied q<0.05 in 85% of random partitions, whereas no other marker demonstrated reliable incremental predictive value. Because the fitted model is an inspectable table of thresholds and class-conditional probabilities, these results identify I-309/CCL1 as an interpretable candidate feature for tabular prediction of cognitive impairment, pending external validation.

</details>


### [277] [FCx: An algorithm for finding Feasible Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19383)
*Kleopatra Markou,Vana Kalogeraki,Dimitrios Gunopulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的反事实解释方法——可行反事实解释（FCx），该方法在生成反事实时显式地强制可行性，同时兼顾现实性、低成本和稀疏性，是首个能高效生成兼具现实、低成本和可行反事实的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反事实解释方法虽然能产生现实且低成本的反事实，但往往无法确保可行性，它们可能建议不可实际执行的修改（如改变一个人的种族以获得工作机会），或与未来的变化不兼容，因此亟需一种显式强制可行性的反事实生成方法。

Method: FCx方法基于改进的变分自编码器（VAE），采用多因子损失函数优化。它同时支持硬可行约束（由领域知识用户指定）和软可行约束（通过因果推理从数据集中自动推断）。成本度量基于数值的绝对变化（邻近性）和变化的特征数量（稀疏性），现实性通过LOF密度估计度量，确保反事实处于数据密集区域。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在多个指标上与现有最优方法表现相当，同时保证了可行性。

Conclusion: FCx是首个能够在保证可行性的前提下，同时实现现实、低成本和稀疏反事实解释的高效方法，填补了现有反事实解释方法在可行性保证上的空白。

Abstract: Counterfactual (CF) explanations identify changes that alter an input's classification. While existing methods produce realistic and low-cost CFs, they often fail to ensure feasibility, by suggesting non-constructive modifications or incompatible with future changes (e.g., changing an individual's race to secure a job offer). We introduce a refinement of CF explanations that explicitly enforces feasibility. Our approach is the first to efficiently generate CFs that are realistic, low-cost and feasible. We accommodate both hard feasible constraints, specified by domain knowledge users, and soft feasible constraints, inferred automatically via causal inference from the dataset. Our method, Feasible Counterfactual Explanations (FCx), is based on a modified Variational Autoencoder (VAE) optimized with a multi-factor loss function. We measure the cost of a change based on the absolute change in values (proximity) as well as the number of features changed (sparsity) while realism is measured based on the LOF for density estimation, guaranteeing that CFs reside in densely populated regions. Extensive experiments on four public datasets show that our approach matches state-of-the-art performance across multiple metrics while guaranteeing feasibility.

</details>


### [278] [Improving Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning via Selective Reward Stimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19414)
*Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出奖励刺激隐式Q学习（RSIQL），一种针对离线目标条件强化学习中稀疏奖励和长时程依赖问题的简单非分层方法。它通过辅助目标条件价值函数识别轨迹中可推进目标进展的中间状态，并在这些状态施加额外奖励信号以减少训练监督的延迟。在D4RL目标到达基准和OGBench上，RSIQL优于目标条件IQL，并与分层离线目标条件方法性能相当，同时保持简单的扁平策略结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离线设置下，目标条件强化学习面临稀疏奖励与长时程依赖的挑战：目标完成信息在时间上远离早期决策，而离线价值估计会引入额外误差。作者从奖励传播视角出发，在简化延迟目标设置中揭示了目标导向的价值分离可能相对于局部估计误差变得很小，从而影响学习效果。

Method: RSIQL是一种非分层方法。它利用辅助的目标条件价值函数来识别被估计为能推进目标进展的中间状态，并对这些状态施加"奖励刺激"（额外奖励信号），从而为训练提供延迟更少的监督信号。与分层方法不同，RSIQL不学习独立的高层子目标策略。

Result: 在D4RL目标到达基准和OGBench上的实验表明，RSIQL在平均性能上优于目标条件IQL，且与分层离线目标条件方法竞争性相当，同时保留了简单的扁平策略结构。

Conclusion: RSIQL通过延迟奖励的刺激机制有效缓解了离线目标条件强化学习中稀疏奖励与长时程依赖带来的价值分离问题，在不引入复杂分层架构的前提下取得了与分层方法相当的性能，验证了从奖励传播角度处理该问题的有效性。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning aims to learn policies that reach specified goals, but remains challenging in offline settings with sparse rewards and long-horizon dependencies. In such settings, goal-completion information can be temporally distant from the early decisions that enable success, while offline value estimation introduces additional error. We study this issue from a reward-propagation perspective and show, in a stylized delayed-goal setting, how goal-directed value separation can become small relative to local estimation error. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Reward Stimulation Implicit Q-Learning (RSIQL), a simple non-hierarchical method that introduces additional reward signals at progress-making intermediate states in offline trajectories. RSIQL uses an auxiliary goal-conditioned value function to identify intermediate states estimated to make progress toward the goal and applies reward stimulation to provide less-delayed training supervision. Unlike hierarchical methods, RSIQL does not learn a separate high-level subgoal policy. Experiments on D4RL goal-reaching benchmarks and OGBench show that RSIQL improves over goal-conditioned IQL on average and achieves performance competitive with hierarchical offline goal-conditioned methods, while retaining a simple flat policy structure.

</details>


### [279] [Bayesian Optimization with Rich Auxiliary Information via LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19437)
*Tejus Gupta,Efe Mert Karagözlü,Rohit Sonker,Barnabás Póczos,Jeff Schnieder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明大语言模型（LLM）能够利用丰富的辅助信息（如训练曲线、专家笔记、图像、先验知识）来指导贝叶斯优化（BO），并提出了三种将辅助信息融入BO的方法。在超参数优化基准和真实核聚变优化任务上，这些方法一致优于标准BO和现有基于LLM的优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化虽广泛用于昂贵黑盒函数优化，但多数实际优化问题包含比单纯函数评估更丰富的信息（如训练曲线、专家笔记、图像、最优位置先验知识），而传统BO方法无法有效利用这些信息。作者旨在让LLM利用这些丰富辅助信息来提升优化效率。

Method: 作者首先验证LLM能够有效利用丰富的辅助信息指导优化，并基于此发现开发了三种将辅助信息纳入贝叶斯优化框架的方法（利用LLM将文本、图像等辅助信息编码或转化为先验/引导信号注入BO流程）。

Result: 在超参数优化基准测试和一个真实核聚变优化任务中，三种方法均一致地优于标准贝叶斯优化和现有基于LLM的优化方法，验证了LLM在利用丰富辅助信息方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究展示了LLM在贝叶斯优化中利用丰富辅助信息的显著能力，为将非数值型、多模态辅助信息融入高效黑盒优化提供了可行且有效的途径。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) is widely used for optimizing expensive black-box functions, yet many real-world optimization problems contain substantially richer information than function evaluations alone. Examples include training curves in hyperparameter optimization, expert notes and images in scientific experimentation, and prior knowledge about where optima may lie. We show that large language models (LLMs) can effectively leverage such rich auxiliary information to guide optimization. Motivated by these findings, we develop three methods for incorporating auxiliary information into BO using LLMs. Across hyperparameter optimization benchmarks and a real-world nuclear fusion optimization task, our methods consistently outperform both standard BO and existing LLM-based optimization approaches. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of LLMs for leveraging rich auxiliary information in BO.

</details>


### [280] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) Improves Classification Accuracy of Bacterial Raman Spectral Data Enabling Portable Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19453)
*Kaitlin Zareno,Jarett Dewbury,Siamak K. Sorooshyari,Hossein Mobahi,Loza F. Tadesse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将锐度感知最小化（SAM）优化器应用于基于拉曼光谱的临床细菌分离物分类任务，以解决传统算法在有限数据集上泛化能力差、依赖复杂预处理的问题。实验表明，相比Adam优化器，SAM在单个数据划分上精度提升最高达10.5%，全部分割的平均精度提升2.7%，展现出推动AI拉曼光谱工具临床应用的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗菌药物耐药性预计到2050年每年将导致1000万人死亡，资源有限地区受影响最严重。拉曼光谱作为新型病原体诊断方法，可在数小时内完成抗生素耐药性快速检测（金标准需数天），但现有算法存在两大局限：1）在跨多样化患者群体的有限数据集上泛化能力不足；2）因需特征提取等非平凡预处理步骤以缓解拉曼光谱数据低质量特性，导致复杂度增加。本文旨在解决这些局限性。

Method: 采用锐度感知最小化（SAM）优化器替代传统Adam优化器，增强模型在临床细菌分离物分类任务中的泛化能力。方法核心是通过寻找损失景观中平坦极小值（而非尖锐极小值）来提升鲁棒性，从而在多样化超参数配置下均能表现良好，并规避对复杂预处理步骤的依赖。

Result: 在光谱分类任务中，SAM相比Adam优化器在单个数据划分上实现最高10.5%的准确率提升，全部分割的平均准确率提升2.7%。

Conclusion: 结果验证了SAM能够有效提升拉曼光谱分类模型在多样化临床场景下的泛化能力，有望推进AI驱动的拉曼光谱工具在临床抗生素耐药性检测中的实际应用。

Abstract: Antimicrobial resistance is expected to claim 10 million lives per year by 2050, and resource-limited regions are most affected. Raman spectroscopy is a novel pathogen diagnostic approach promising rapid and portable antibiotic resistance testing within a few hours, compared to days when using gold standard methods. However, current algorithms for Raman spectra analysis 1) are unable to generalize well on limited datasets across diverse patient populations and 2) require increased complexity due to the necessity of non-trivial pre-processing steps, such as feature extraction, which are essential to mitigate the low-quality nature of Raman spectral data. In this work, we address these limitations using Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) to enhance model generalization across a diverse array of hyperparameters in clinical bacterial isolate classification tasks. We demonstrate that SAM achieves accuracy improvements of up to 10.5% on a single split, and an increase in average accuracy of 2.7% across all splits in spectral classification tasks over the traditional optimizer, Adam. These results display the capability of SAM to advance the clinical application of AI-powered Raman spectroscopy tools.

</details>


### [281] [Enhanced Agriculture-informed Neural Network by Domain Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19466)
*Ci Lin,Futong Li,Rose Chong-Wu,Tet Yeap,Iluju Kiringa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出知识增强的农业信息神经网络（KAINN），一种结合领域知识（肥料扩散、土壤呼吸、充水孔隙度）的混合神经-机理框架，用于预测农业N2O排放。实验表明KAINN在CNN、LSTM、Transformer架构下相比纯数据驱动模型和原始AINN具有更低的RMSE、MAE和更高的R²，且学习到的界面参数轨迹更平滑、物理一致性更好、不确定性更低，提升了预测的可靠性、可解释性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业N2O排放预测对环境影响评估和可持续农业至关重要，但排放由土壤性质、气候、生化过程和管理措施等复杂交互决定，且高质量观测数据有限。纯深度学习模型虽能捕捉非线性关系，但缺乏物理可解释性且跨环境泛化能力差。

Method: 提出KAINN混合神经-机理框架，在农业信息神经网络（AINN）基础上融入肥料扩散、土壤呼吸和水填充孔隙度等领域知识，并在CNN、LSTM和Transformer三种架构上、多个生长季节及多种输入特征配置下进行系统评估。

Result: KAINN普遍获得比纯数据驱动模型和原始AINN更低的均方根误差（RMSE）、平均绝对误差（MAE）和更高的决定系数（R²）。对学习到的界面分析显示参数轨迹更平滑、物理一致性更强且不确定性降低。

Conclusion: 将环境知识融入神经网络可提高农业N2O排放预测的可靠性、可解释性和泛化能力，为混合建模在农业环境预测中的应用提供了有力证据。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of nitrous oxide (N2O) emissions from agriculture is important for assessing environmental impacts and supporting sustainable farming. However, prediction remains difficult because N2O emissions result from complex interactions among soil properties, climate, biochemical processes, and management practices, while high-quality observations are limited. Deep learning models can capture nonlinear relationships but often lack physical interpretability and may generalize poorly across environmental conditions. We propose the Knowledge-enhanced Agriculture-informed Neural Network (KAINN), a hybrid neural-mechanistic framework that extends the Agriculture-informed Neural Network by incorporating domain knowledge about fertilizer diffusion, soil respiration, and water-filled porosity. We evaluate KAINN using CNN, LSTM, and Transformer architectures across multiple growing seasons and input-feature configurations. The results show that KAINN generally provides lower root mean square error and mean absolute error and higher R-squared values than purely data-driven models and the original AINN. Analysis of the learned interfaces also shows smoother and more physically consistent parameter trajectories with reduced uncertainty. These findings demonstrate that incorporating environmental knowledge into neural networks can improve the reliability, interpretability, and generalization of agricultural N2O-emission predictions.

</details>


### [282] [Search at the Cost of Sampling: Nearly-Instant Latent Space Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19476)
*Donney Fan,Colin Doumont,Aleksandra Kalisz,Paul Duckworth,Jacob R. Gardner,Henry Moss,Geoff Pleiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将贝叶斯优化（BO）实用化应用于从头设计发现流程的方法。通过利用线性模型在球形域（高维潜在变量集中区域）上的独特组合，推导出近似闭式解来解决代理建模和采集问题，实现了比最先进基线至少100倍的加速，同时在分子和图像生成基准上保持或提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在从头设计发现管线的核心位置越来越重要，设计以大规模生成并通过虚拟筛选来确定候选集。贝叶斯优化天然适合该场景（利用过往评估引导未来提案），但其顺序决策的计算开销在虚拟筛选相对廉价时成为瓶颈，因此需要使BO在此场景下变得实用。

Method: 利用线性模型与球形域的独特组合（高维潜在变量在此域集中），借鉴近期关于线性代理模型合理性的工作，同时推导出利用球形对称性的近似闭式解，用以解决代理建模和采集问题。

Result: 相比最先进基线实现了至少100倍的加速，并且在分子和图像生成基准上性能相当或更优。

Conclusion: 该方法使贝叶斯优化成为从头设计管线中切实可用的即插即用组件，解决了此前因速度太慢而无法纳入该流程的问题。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly central to many de novo discovery pipelines, in which designs are generated at scale and filtered through virtual screens to determine a set of candidates to experimentally validate. While Bayesian optimization (BO) is a natural fit for this setting, as it uses past evaluations to guide future proposals, the computational overhead required for its sequential decision-making becomes a bottleneck when virtual screens are relatively cheap. We make BO practical in this regime by exploiting the unique combination of a linear model constrained to a spherical domain where high-dimensional latents concentrate. We build off recent work justifying the use of linear surrogates, while deriving nearly closed-form solutions to the surrogate modelling and acquisition problems that exploit spherical symmetry. The result is at least a 100x speedup over state-of-the art baselines, with matching or improved performance across molecular and image generation benchmarks. Altogether, our method makes BO a practical drop-in for de novo pipelines where it was previously too slow to consider.

</details>


### [283] [Sample Count Is Not Enough: Candidate-Generation Strategy Shapes the Energy and Performance of LLM Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19499)
*Mobina Kashaniyan,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示测试时采样推理的性能（如候选数N、准确率）并不能反映系统开销，因为相同候选预算可经不同生成调度（单次批处理 vs 多次串行）执行。实验表明固定候选数N=8时，8次串行调用相较1次批量调用会消耗4.64-4.86倍的GPU设备能量、产生5.77-6.12倍的P95延迟，因此评测应同时报告候选数、准确率、生成调度及GPU级系统指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样式测试时缩放通过生成并组合多个候选响应来提升大语言模型推理能力，通常用候选数N描述推理预算。但N只说明生成多少候选，无法反映候选如何执行（单次批量生成或拆分为多个小批量串行调用）。作者旨在指出仅凭N无法刻画多候选测试时缩放的系统代价，并系统量化不同生成调度的效率差异。

Method: 先研究增大N（1至8）对推理准确率的影响，使用Phi-3-mini和Qwen2.5-1.5B在500条GSM8K提示上评测。随后固定N=8，对比四种生成调度1x8、2x4、4x2、8x1（axb表示a次生成调用、每次b个候选），在保持总候选数不变的前提下测量延迟、吞吐量、GPU小时及GPU设备总能量。实验在A100 GPU上进行，并以SciQ/V100短输出实验及三个独立调度的A100节点上分别复现，验证结论的稳健性。

Result: 增大N从1到8使Phi-3-mini准确率提升8.4个百分点、Qwen2.5-1.5B提升18.4个百分点。固定N=8时，8次串行调用相对1次批量调用，在A100上消耗4.64-4.86倍的GPU设备总能量，P95延迟为5.77-6.12倍。该规律在三种独立调度节点的两个模型及SciQ/V100短输出实验中一致出现，说明候选数单调增长带来的准确率优势掩盖了显著的串行调度系统开销。

Conclusion: 候选数N不足以描述多候选测试时缩放的系统成本。当候选彼此独立且内存允许时，以更大批量、更少生成调用（即更倾向于单次批处理）更加高效。评测工作不应仅报告候选数与准确率，还须报告生成调度方式及GPU级系统指标（能量、延迟、吞吐）等，以便全面评估测试时缩放的真实开销。

Abstract: Test-time scaling can improve large language model reasoning by generating and combining multiple candidate responses. In sampling-based methods, the inference budget is often described by the number of generated candidates, N. However, N tells us how many candidates are generated, not how they are executed. The same candidate budget can be produced in one batched generation call or split across several sequential calls with smaller batch sizes. We first study the effect of increasing N on reasoning accuracy using Phi-3-mini and Qwen2.5-1.5B on 500 GSM8K prompts. As expected, increasing N from 1 to 8 improves accuracy by 8.4 percentage points for Phi-3-mini and 18.4 points for Qwen2.5-1.5B. However, accuracy alone does not show the systems cost of using a larger candidate budget. We therefore fix N = 8 and compare four generation schedules: 1x8, 2x4, 4x2, and 8x1, where axb denotes a generation calls with b candidates per call. We measure latency, throughput, GPU-hours, and gross GPU-device energy while keeping the total candidate count fixed. On A100 GPUs, eight serial calls use 4.64-4.86x as much gross GPU-device energy and have 5.77-6.12x the P95 latency of one batched call with eight candidates. The same pattern appears across three independently scheduled A100 nodes per model and in short-output SciQ/V100 experiments. These results show that candidate count alone is not enough to describe the systems cost of multi-candidate test-time scaling. When candidates are independent and memory allows it, fewer generation calls with larger batch sizes are more efficient. Evaluations should therefore report not only candidate count and accuracy, but also generation schedule and GPU-level systems metrics.

</details>


### [284] [LSTM-UT and Recurrent-Depth Transformers on Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19521)
*Aras Kavuncu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种递归深度Transformer变体：仅携带当前隐藏状态的块通用Transformer（BUT）、保留不断扩展注意力缓存的CoTFormer、以及具有有界门控记忆的新型LSTM通用Transformer（LSTM-UT）。实验表明，在Rule 30元胞自动机上BUT比CoTFormer更可靠地外推到未见的递归深度；在延迟召回任务中BUT同样优于CoTFormer；而LSTM-UT在深度外推和延迟召回两方面均优于两种基线，支持有界门控记忆作为重复计算和后期检索的有效归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归深度Transformer共享计算但保留信息的方式不同，作者旨在系统比较不同记忆/缓存机制（仅当前状态、扩展注意力缓存、有界门控记忆）在重复计算（深度外推）和跨步信息检索（延迟召回）任务上的表现差异，并探究其失败机制。

Method: 在Rule 30元胞自动机上比较BUT、CoTFormer与LSTM-UT的深度外推能力；通过状态与缓存干预实验剖析CoTFormer失败的原因（当前状态与保留历史的交互作用）；在延迟召回任务中考察各模型对过往状态的选择性访问能力；LSTM-UT作为带边界门控记忆的新基线提出并验证。

Result: BUT较CoTFormer更可靠地外推到未见递归深度，但精度最终会退化；CoTFormer的失败源于当前状态与保留历史的交互——修正当前状态可暂时恢复精度，但保留的历史会破坏该修正；在延迟召回任务中BUT同样优于CoTFormer（后者无法可靠选择所需缓存表示）；LSTM-UT在两项任务上均优于两种基线。

Conclusion: 结果支持有界门控记忆是重复计算与后期检索任务的有效归纳偏置：相比不断膨胀的注意力缓存，受限且可门控的记忆在控制历史对当前状态干扰的同时，又能提供可访问的过往表示，从而在深度外推与延迟召回上取得更优的均衡。

Abstract: Recurrent-depth Transformers apply shared computation repeatedly, but differ in how they retain information across steps. We compare a Block Universal Transformer (BUT), which carries only its current hidden state; CoTFormer, which also retains an expanding attention cache; and a new LSTM Universal Transformer (LSTM-UT) with bounded gated memory. On Rule 30 cellular automata, BUT extrapolates to unseen recurrent depths more reliably than CoTFormer, although its accuracy eventually degrades. State and cache interventions show that CoTFormer's failure depends on their interaction: correcting the current state can temporarily restore accuracy, while retained history can undermine that correction. In a delayed-recall task, BUT also outperforms CoTFormer despite lacking direct access to past states; CoTFormer does not reliably select the requested cached representation. LSTM-UT improves both depth extrapolation and delayed recall over these baselines. The results support bounded gated memory as an effective inductive bias for repeated computation and later retrieval in these tasks.

</details>


### [285] [Compressed Active Subspaces for Scalable Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19539)
*Thomas Flynn,Sanket Jantre,Byung-Jun Yoon,Kibaek Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出压缩活动子空间（CAS）方法，通过结构化等距嵌入将模型参数映射到压缩空间，再在该降维参数化中构建活动子空间，从而大幅降低高维模型（如大型神经网络）中活动子空间构建所需的内存，使其能够在标准方法不可行的大模型上进行贝叶斯推理，同时保持预测性能和稳健的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活动子空间方法通过识别对模型输出影响最大的参数方向来进行高维模型的不确定性量化，但其构建过程需要存储大量全维模型梯度，随着模型规模增大，内存开销变得不可接受，限制了其在大模型上的应用。

Method: 提出压缩活动子空间（Compressed Active Subspaces, CAS）：首先利用结构化等距嵌入将模型参数映射到压缩空间，然后在该降维参数化空间中构建活动子空间，从而在显著降低内存需求的同时完成后续的贝叶斯推理。

Result: 在规模不断增大的神经网络上验证了CAS的可扩展性，在保持预测性能与稳健不确定性估计的同时，大幅减少了活动子空间构建所需的内存，使标准活动子空间方法不可行的大模型推理成为可能。

Conclusion: CAS成功解决了活动子空间方法在大模型上的内存瓶颈，为高维模型的可扩展不确定性量化与贝叶斯推理提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Active subspace methods provide a framework for quantifying predictive uncertainty in high-dimensional models by identifying and performing inference along parameter directions that have the greatest influence on the model output. However, the construction of active subspaces requires storing many full-dimensional model gradients, which becomes prohibitive as model size increases. We address this limitation by proposing Compressed Active Subspaces (CAS), a scalable approach that first maps the model parameters to a compressed space using a structured isometric embedding and then constructs the active subspace within this reduced parameterization. Our approach substantially reduces the memory required for active subspace construction and enables Bayesian inference for large models where standard active subspace methods become impractical. We demonstrate the scalability of CAS on neural networks of increasing size while maintaining predictive performance and robust uncertainty estimates.

</details>


### [286] [FedFIbOS: Fisher Importance based Optimal Submodelling for Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19559)
*Yasmeen Afzal,Jeremiah D. Deng,Haibo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedFIbOS方法，利用Fisher信息（而非经验性参数幅度）作为子模型参数选择准则，用于异构联邦学习。该方法从最小化子模型掩蔽误差出发，理论性地推导出Fisher加权的二次掩蔽代理，并证明原始Fisher top-k规则可求解该代理。通过平方梯度高效估计经验对角Fisher信息，无需额外优化开销。在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和AGNews上，病态及Dirichlet非IID设置下，相比现有最优方法准确率提升约10%，且在更强异构性下提升更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构联邦学习方法使用启发式重要性度量（主要是参数幅度）选择子模型参数，但这些选择标准缺乏收敛框架下的理论依据。作者识别出两个关键缺口：其一，现有参数选择准则缺乏基于收敛框架的理论支撑；其二，部分客户端参与会在Fisher分数中引入额外的估计效应。这导致现有方法无法保证收敛性能。

Method: 提出FedFIbOS框架：以Fisher信息作为原则性准则，从最小化子模型掩蔽误差出发推导。理论上将子模型选择建模为Fisher加权的二次掩蔽代理问题，证明在Fisher主导排序条件下，FedFIbOS采用的原始Fisher top-k规则可求解该代理。方法保留了底层掩蔽联邦优化界的收敛结构。Fisher分数通过平方梯度高效估计经验对角Fisher信息，实现稳定自适应的参数选择，无需额外优化开销。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和AGNews数据集上，病态和Dirichlet非IID设置下的实验表明，FedFIbOS相比现有最优方法准确率提升约10%，且改进幅度在更强的数据异构性下更加明显。

Conclusion: FedFIbOS填补了异构联邦子模型参数选择缺乏理论依据的空白，确立了Fisher信息优于启发式幅度准则的理论地位，提出了一种高效、稳定且自适应、无需额外开销的参数选择方法。该方法不仅保留了掩蔽联邦优化界的收敛结构，还在强异构场景下显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Heterogeneous federated learning requires clients with diverse computational capacities to collaboratively train a global model, where each client trains a capacity-constrained submodel. Existing methods select submodel parameters using heuristic importance measures---most prominently parameter magnitude---without theoretical justification for why these measures support convergence. We identify a fundamental gap: existing parameter selection criteria lack theoretical grounding in the convergence framework, partial client participation introduces additional estimation effects in the Fisher scores. We propose \textbf{FedFIbOS}: Fisher Importance-based Optimal Submodelling for heterogeneous federated learning, using Fisher Information in a principled criterion derived from minimizing submodel masking error. %We formally establish when magnitude selection is equivalent to Fisher selection fail under non-IID heterogeneous federated learning. We theoretically formulate submodel selection through a Fisher-weighted quadratic masking surrogate and show that the raw Fisher top-$k$ rule implemented by FedFIbOS solves this surrogate under a Fisher-dominant ranking condition. The resulting method retains the convergence structure of the underlying masked federated optimization bound. Fisher scores are efficiently estimated from empirical diagonal Fisher information using squared gradients, enabling stable and adaptive parameter selection without additional optimization overhead. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and AGNews under pathological and Dirichlet non-IID settings show FedFIbOS achieves ${\approx}10\%$ higher accuracy than the state of the art, with improvements becoming more pronounced under stronger heterogeneity.

</details>


### [287] [The Complexity Kink: A Prompt-Side Structural Complexity Index for Code-Generation Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19616)
*Michael Hernandez,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个生成前、独立于正确性的六维度提示词结构复杂度指数（prompt-side structural-complexity index），在5,000个Python提示词、21个模型（共105,000次生成）上进行评分与评估，发现通过率在复合分数13.75处存在非单调断点，但该断点随任务类型和构造框架漂移且不具普遍性，最终将该指数作为测量框架提供有界观测分析而非因果解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从生成代码测得的复杂度依赖失败结果：一个困难的提示词可能生成一个短小且失败的代码，从而被赋予低的输出复杂度。因此需要一种在生成之前、与正确性分离的提示词侧复杂度测量，以可靠刻画提示词难度。

Method: 构建六维度结构复杂度指数，在生成前评分并与正确性分离。先用单评分者的初步评分表选出5,000个Python提示词，横跨六个分数带；再由4名外部LLM评分者对锁定提示词重新评分，得到19,997行评分（4,998个提示词有全部4个评分），复合组内信度ICC=0.872。每个提示词由21个模型评测，共105,000次生成。采用未调整的均值合并分析、任务类型固定效应、构造框架对照、模型特定拟合、365提示词审计清理扩展、零通过子样本（对比Lizard输出复杂度）以及人类评分一致性、释义与跨语言重评分等检验。

Result: 未调整均值合并分析中，通过率在复合分数13.75处出现非单调断点：13.75及以下为79.9%，以上为87.6%，但并非普遍失败阈值。加入任务类型固定效应后断点移至10.75，机制差距由7.6个百分点缩至2.1；构造框架对照将其移至8.50，原始差距为-3.5个百分点，单靠任一框架都无法复现合并下的+7.6点变化。模型特定拟合有16个上行、5个下行变化。365提示词审计清理扩展在bins 15和16与原五模型估计一致，但仅在bin 16以上新增14个提示词。零通过子样本中，28.5%的生成其提示词复合分数高于8而输出复杂度至多为10。人类评分一致性呈中等且依赖评分者；释义与跨语言重评分保持分数排序。过度识别检验拒绝六维度的联合约束，因此将复合分数视为指数，不对2SLS估计作因果解释。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于提供一个生成前的测量框架以及对可靠性机制的有界观测分析；该复合指数可作为测量工具，但其断点随任务类型与框架漂移，且不具因果解释力，需谨慎使用。

Abstract: Complexity measured from generated code is failure-dependent: a difficult prompt can yield a short failing program and be assigned low output complexity. We introduce a six-dimension prompt-side structural-complexity index scored before generation and kept separate from correctness. We select 5,000 Python prompts across six bands of a preliminary single-rater rubric. Four out-of-panel LLM raters rescore the locked prompts, giving 19,997 score rows; composite inter-rater reliability is ICC = 0.872 on the 4,998 prompts with all four ratings. We evaluate 21 models per prompt, yielding 105,000 generations. In the unadjusted mean-pooled analysis, pass rate has a nonmonotone breakpoint at composite 13.75, with 79.9% at or below and 87.6% above. This is not a universal failure cutoff. Task-type fixed effects shift the breakpoint to 10.75 and cut the regime gap from 7.6 to 2.1 points. A construction-frame control shifts it to 8.50 with a raw gap of -3.5 points, and neither frame alone reproduces the pooled +7.6-point change. Model-specific fits include 16 upward and five downward changes. A 365-prompt audit-clean extension matches the original five-model estimates at bins 15 and 16 but adds only 14 prompts above bin 16. Among zero-pass generations with computable Lizard complexity, 28.5% pair a prompt composite above 8 with output complexity at most 10. Human agreement is moderate and rater-dependent on a disagreement-enriched calibration set; paraphrase and cross-language rescoring preserve score ordering. Overidentification tests reject the joint restrictions on the six dimensions, so we treat the composite as an index and make no causal interpretation of the 2SLS estimates. The contribution is a pre-generation measurement framework and a bounded observational analysis of reliability regimes.

</details>


### [288] [A Policy Profile for Croissant: Refusal as a Property of the Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19640)
*Alexander Chernov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为 Croissant（ML 数据集的事实标准机器可读描述符）补上了缺失的"求值语义"：提出了一个可加性（additive）配置文件，让数据集在封闭的五个算子上声明其允许的操作及条件，并给出完整决策过程，使网关仅凭描述符即可裁决并记录检查项，同时处理不可求值条件、调用方权限组合等此前版本从未规定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Croissant 1.1 起推荐用 DUO 和 ODRL 承载数据使用条件，但任何版本都没有规定这些条件如何被求值：没有决策过程、没有求值成本上界、没有"实现无法求值"时的结果、没有对已检查内容的记录，也没有与调用方（caller-side）权限的组合规则。作者旨在补全这"另一半"。

Method: 定义一个可加性配置文件，让数据集在封闭的五个算子集上声明"它允许的操作"及"允许的条件"，并给出完整决策过程；网关仅依据描述符即可裁决并记录所检查的内容。通过两个语料库（corpora）评估，证据彼此隔离：一个由配置文件的文法生成的语料覆盖全部算子、拒绝类和一致性条款；另一个来自真实 nf-core 流水线门控的部署结果。

Result: 用真实 nf-core 流水线门控的三个描述符给出的部署结果：配置文件文档的决策与网关原生描述符逐条记录一致；剥离该层后仍是合法 Croissant 文档；新增成本为 11.7μs，对比 119μs 的基础决策耗时。文法生成语料覆盖每个算子、每种拒绝类与一致性条款；在全部合法案例中，552 份完整决策记录在三个维度（原生描述符、配置文件术语、用 usageInfo 中的 ODRL 表达同一策略）上达成一致。

Conclusion: 载体（carrier，即采用的 ODRL/DUO 等格式或配置层）并非本文的贡献，求值语义才是。此外，调用方绑定（caller-bound）与数据绑定（data-bound）的策略作用于互不重叠的状态空间，因此两者许可集互不包含，说明二者必须组合而非可互相替代。

Abstract: Croissant is the de facto machine-readable descriptor for ML datasets: JSON-LD over schema.org. Since version 1.1 it also carries data use conditions, recommending DUO and ODRL for them. What no version specifies is how any of them is evaluated: no decision procedure, no bound on evaluation cost, no outcome for a condition an implementation cannot evaluate, no record of what was checked, and nothing on composition with caller-side authority. We supply that half. An additive profile lets a dataset declare the operations it admits and the conditions under which it admits them, over a closed set of five operators whose decision procedure is given in full, so a gate decides from the descriptor alone and records what it checked. Two corpora evaluate it and their evidence is kept apart. Three descriptors that gated a real nf-core pipeline give the deployment result: decisions from a profile document match the gate's native descriptor record for record, stripping the layer leaves a valid Croissant document, and the added cost is 11.7 $μ$s against a 119 $μ$s decision. A corpus generated from the profile's grammar gives the breadth, covering every operator, refusal class and conformance clause. Across its valid cases, 552 complete decision records agree three ways -- native descriptor, profile terms, and the same policy as ODRL in usageInfo. The carrier is therefore not the contribution; the evaluation semantics is. Finally, caller-bound and data-bound policies range over non-overlapping state spaces, so neither permit set contains the other.

</details>


### [289] [CoRe: Coherence and Relational Alignment for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19670)
*Xiaoyu Lin,Huiran Duan,Yining Liu,Zhixiang Wu,Chu Lin,Lin Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoRe是一个模型无关的多元时序直接预测学习目标，它用两种输出空间约束（频率一致性损失和低秩关系图损失）替代逐点误差（如MSE）监督，在不增加可训练参数、仅需替换损失函数的情况下，提升现有预测主干在不同基准上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接预测（direct forecasting）已成为多元时序预测的标准范式，因为它能单次预测完整未来时域。但其训练目标常被分解为逐点误差（如MSE）。这类目标提供稳定的监督，但并未显式保留未来轨迹的结构：单变量内部的时间连贯性以及跨变量的关系一致性都可能被削弱。

Method: CoRe通过两个输出空间约束替换逐点监督：(1) 频率一致性损失，用于对齐预测谱与目标谱；(2) 低秩关系图损失，用于在目标导出的PCA子空间中匹配采样的成对差异。该目标不引入任何可训练参数，只需更换损失函数即可应用于现有预测主干。

Result: 在标准基准上的实验表明，CoRe改善了强基线，相比近期提出的预测目标具有竞争力，并且在不同主干、数据集和超参数设置下整体保持一致的有效性。

Conclusion: CoRe证明了仅通过引入结构化输出空间约束（而非增加模型复杂度或参数）就能提升多元直接预测的效果，是一种轻量、通用且稳健的训练目标改进方案。

Abstract: Direct forecasting has become a standard paradigm for multivariate time-series forecasting because it predicts the full future horizon in a single pass. However, its training objective is often still decomposed into pointwise errors such as MSE. Such objectives provide stable supervision, but they do not explicitly preserve the structure of the future trajectory: temporal coherence within each variable and relational consistency across variables can both be weakened. We propose CoRe, a model-agnostic learning objective for direct multivariate forecasting. CoRe replaces pointwise supervision with two output-space constraints: a frequency coherence loss that aligns predicted and target spectra, and a low-rank relational graph loss that matches sampled pairwise differences in a target-derived PCA subspace. The resulting objective introduces no trainable parameters and can be applied to existing forecasting backbones by changing only the loss. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that CoRe improves strong baselines, compares favorably with recent forecasting objectives, and remains effective across different backbones, datasets, and hyperparameter settings overall consistently.

</details>


### [290] [Conservation Buys Stability and Factoring Buys Counterfactuals in Physical World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19674)
*Yufeng Wang,Parivesh Priye,Lu Wei,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG
